Weakest: Product Knowledge (2.9/10)

Agents fail to explain Standard vs Classified vs Hot Vacancy. Most calls lack feature-level articulation. Immediate training needed.

Strongest: Tone (7.7/10)

Agents are consistently polite and professional — strong foundation to build on.

Key Findings

What's Working Well

  • Maintained respectful and professional tone throughout (51)
  • Clear introduction with company identification (41)
  • Professional and courteous closing (39)
  • Captured key contact details (email/phone) for database (26)
  • Good needs discovery — asked about hiring requirements, location, and volume (25)
  • Demonstrated product knowledge — explained plan features clearly (22)

Critical Gaps

  • Did not confirm if speaking to decision maker — risk of wasted pitch (44)
  • Skipped needs discovery — should ask about hiring volume, profiles, locations be (41)
  • No follow-up action set — call may lead nowhere without WhatsApp/callback (33)
  • Did not pitch any plans — missed opportunity to present Naukri's solutions (30)
  • No value proposition communicated — should highlight candidate quality and reach (28)
  • Missing proper introduction — should clearly state name and 'calling from Naukri (25)

Call Outcome Distribution

General Discussion 22 (33%) Potential Conversion 17 (26%) Callback Scheduled 15 (23%) WhatsApp Follow-up 8 (12%) Free Posting Discussed 4 (6%)

Actionable Recommendations

1. Product Knowledge Workshop (This Week)

Score 2.9/10. Agents can't differentiate Standard/Classified/Hot Vacancy. Run 2-hr session with plan comparison matrix and role-play exercises.

2. Mandatory Process Checklist

Score 3.9/10. Implement: (1) Confirm decision maker, (2) Capture email, (3) WhatsApp details, (4) Set callback. Track compliance daily.

3. Needs Discovery Before Pitch

Many calls jump to plans without understanding volume, location, or urgency. Train: 3-5 discovery questions before any product mention.

4. Close Every Call with Next Step

23 of 66 calls ended without next step. Every call must result in: purchase, callback date, WhatsApp follow-up, or documented rejection.

5. Use Free Job Posting as Default Fallback

When prospect says "not interested," offer free posting as door-opener. Only 16 agents used this — make it standard for every rejection.

Data Quality Note

140 raw recordings: 74 filtered (40 failed "Hinglish" transcriptions, 25 garbled Devanagari encoding, 6 voicemails, 2 too-short, 1 generic summary). Improving the Hindi/Hinglish speech-to-text pipeline would nearly double auditable volume.
